# Agents · V · part 2

963 entries, valuation-analyst to ventas. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **valuation-analyst**: 科技项目估值分析——按企业生命周期选择估值路径（实物期权/DCF/相对估值/潜在盈利能力 BMP），输出估值区间与好球对照。MUST BE USED 当需要对项目估值/判断价格合理性时。 ([Justinjchen-Cornell/Hardtech-Venture-Capital-OS](https://github.com/Justinjchen-Cornell/Hardtech-Venture-Capital-OS/blob/HEAD/agents/valuation-analyst.md) · ★2)
- **valuation-analyst**: Use this agent to value a business conservatively — normalized earnings power, at least two independent valuation methods, margin-of-safety calculation, and reverse-engineering the expectations embedded in the current price. All arithmetic computed with tools/code on tool-fetched inputs. Use proactively as part of any deep dive, and when the user asks "what's it worth?", "is it cheap?", or "what does the price imply?". &lt;example&gt; Context: Deep dive in progress. user: "Deep dive on Company Z please" assistant: "The valuation-analyst will build the valuation from tool-fetched figures in parallel… ([Soo0803/wj-capital-agents](https://github.com/Soo0803/wj-capital-agents/blob/HEAD/wj-capital/agents/valuation-analyst.md) · ★2)
- **valuation-analyst**: 估值分析師。執行 P/E、P/S、EV/EBITDA、PEG、Comps（同業比較）五種估值方法。 不做 DCF。Peer group 由估值分析師依產業自行定義，不需委託方指定。 不主動發起分析，只接受 senior-analyst 或 industry-analyst 的委託。 結果存入 data/valuation/[TICKER]-[YYYYMMDD].json。 ([bargisula/bargisula.github.io](https://github.com/bargisula/bargisula.github.io/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/valuation-analyst.md))
- **valuation-analyst**: Chooses bear, base and bull drivers for a ten-year DCF with a reason and a quote or calculator field for each, then — once Python has run the DCF — reads the value range, where the price sits in it, the market-implied growth against history and guidance, the two assumptions value is most sensitive to, and what the accounting adjustments moved. Writes assumptions.json, then analysis_valuation.json. States no recommendation to trade. ([lastwhisper906-gif/aaer-evals](https://github.com/lastwhisper906-gif/aaer-evals/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/valuation-analyst.md))
- **valuation-analyst**: Valuation analyst on the earnings-desk deep-dive desk. Use in Phase 2 of a deep dive (in parallel with the earnings, forensic and industry analysts) to work out what the current price implies. Runs a reverse DCF for implied growth and margin, builds bear/base/bull scenario DCFs, compares peer multiples with growth-adjusted regression, and frames the expectations gap. Writes memos/valuation.md in the run directory. Also usable standalone for "what's priced in?" or "is it overvalued?" ([MozartofCode/Earnings-Intelligence-Agent](https://github.com/MozartofCode/Earnings-Intelligence-Agent/blob/HEAD/plugins/earnings-desk/agents/valuation-analyst.md))
- **valuation-analyst**: Valuation ranges via comparables, DCF, VC method; registered-valuer requirements. Use for pricing a round. ([SaketSinghRajput/AGENTSHQ](https://github.com/SaketSinghRajput/AGENTSHQ/blob/HEAD/teams/cfo/agents/valuation-analyst.md))
- **valuation-critic**: Attacks a finished analysis and reports what breaks. Runs the consistency validator first, then works the red-team sequence — route conformance, bias, single charge per risk, growth paid for, terminal value plausibility, the equity bridge, implied expectations, comparable-set bias, and the seven sins of acquisition analysis. Writes challenge.json and challenge.md, and never edits the artifacts it reviews. Delegate to it once gate G6_valued has passed and before any verdict or report, or whenever a completed valuation needs an adversarial pass before somebody acts on the number. ([lyndonkl/claude](https://github.com/lyndonkl/claude/blob/HEAD/agents/valuation-critic.md) · ★200)
- **valuation-dcf-comps**: Company valuation — DCF, comparable analysis, price targets. Use for: valuation, DCF, comparables, comps, price target, WACC. ([rshetty66/VOLT-Main](https://github.com/rshetty66/VOLT-Main/blob/HEAD/plugins/volt/agents/valuation-dcf-comps.md) · ★1)

- **vbus_bagtest**: **Answer.** Of all the variables tested, **vehicle speed (and the load that comes with it) is the only one that moves the V_bus residual by a large amount**. In fw v37, the residual above about 5 Hz grows from 3.1 mV rms below 0.5 m/s, which is the standstill floor, to 13.5 mV rms at 2.5-3.5 m/s. That is 4.4x. At a fixed speed band, I_tot moves it only 1.2-1.3x. ([Tangineering/DC-Balancer-Board-Teensy](https://github.com/Tangineering/DC-Balancer-Board-Teensy/blob/HEAD/docs/reviews/noise_fw37_2026-11-05/agents/vbus_bagtest.md) · ★1)
- **vbus_blg1k**: **The white floor is the ADC chain, not the bus.** At standstill V_bus carries 0.44-0.46 LSB rms over 50-442 Hz. The quiet V_rgn channel (13.5 V, same ADC) carries 0.49-0.50 LSB, and I_fc/I_batt near zero carry 0.42-0.49 LSB. For reference, 1-LSB quantization is 0.27 LSB, or 62 uV/rtHz at 4.545 mV/LSB (`SCALE_V_BUS`, :2028). With the motor running, broadband regions away from the lines rise only t ([Tangineering/DC-Balancer-Board-Teensy](https://github.com/Tangineering/DC-Balancer-Board-Teensy/blob/HEAD/docs/reviews/noise_fw37_2026-11-05/agents/vbus_blg1k.md) · ★1)
- **vbus_critic**: **Verdict.** The four reports agree that the RT1987s add no measurable V_bus noise. Most of the bag ripple is real speed-locked bus ripple folded down by the 21 ms snapshot, plus the folded ADC floor. TPS61288 switching ripple is at most about 1.2 mV per sample. Several numbers disagree between reports, and quick computations settle each one below. The diode question still has one open part: nobod ([Tangineering/DC-Balancer-Board-Teensy](https://github.com/Tangineering/DC-Balancer-Board-Teensy/blob/HEAD/docs/reviews/noise_fw37_2026-11-05/agents/vbus_critic.md) · ★1)
- **vbus_rt1987**: **Bottom line.** None of the measured V_bus noise comes from the RT1987s. The switches matter in two quieter ways. Each one adds about 10 mOhm of incremental series resistance outside the droop loop, which shifts the incremental load split by about +0.02 toward 0.5. Each one also adds a common 35 mV offset, and that offset cancels in the split. In ML0226 the real V_bus content in 6-24 Hz is only 0 ([Tangineering/DC-Balancer-Board-Teensy](https://github.com/Tangineering/DC-Balancer-Board-Teensy/blob/HEAD/docs/reviews/noise_fw37_2026-11-05/agents/vbus_rt1987.md) · ★1)
- **vbus_switching**: **Answer.** Converter switching ripple explains neither the V_bus standstill floor nor most of the moving excess. - **Standstill floor.** This is the Teensy ADC floor. TPS61288 ripple adds less than 0.1 mV per sample here. - **Moving excess in the 1 kHz BLG.** This splits into two parts: - **61-66 %: a real bus-voltage line.** It is speed-locked at F = 453 Hz per m/s, real at its unfolded frequenc ([Tangineering/DC-Balancer-Board-Teensy](https://github.com/Tangineering/DC-Balancer-Board-Teensy/blob/HEAD/docs/reviews/noise_fw37_2026-11-05/agents/vbus_switching.md) · ★1)
